However, these systems keep their training data closed and remain opaque about their task and trajectory synthesis recipes. We present OpenMobile, an open-source framework that synthesizes high-quality task instructions and agent trajectories, with two key components: (1) The first is a scalable task synthesis pipeline that constructs a global environment memory from exploration, then leverages it to generate diverse and grounded instructions. and (2) a policy-switching strategy for trajectory rollout. By alternating between learner and expert models, it captures essential error-recovery data often missing in standard imitation learning. Agents trained on our data achieve competitive results across three dynamic mobile agent benchmarks: notably, our fine-tuned Qwen2.5-VL and Qwen3-VL reach 51.7% and 64.7% on AndroidWorld, far surpassing existing open-data approaches. Furthermore, we conduct transparent analyses on the overlap between our synthetic instructions and benchmark test sets, and verify that performance gains stem from broad functionality coverage rather than benchmark overfitting. We release data and code at https://njucckevin.github.io/openmobile/ to bridge the data gap and facilitate broader mobile agent research.

</details>


### [116] [HyperSpace: A Generalized Framework for Spatial Encoding in Hyperdimensional Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15113)
*Shay Snyder,Andrew Capodieci,David Gorsich,Maryam Parsa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HyperSpace是一个开源框架，将向量符号架构系统分解为模块化算子，用于编码、绑定、捆绑、相似性、清理和回归，通过该系统分析发现HRR和FHRR在端到端性能上相当，但内存占用存在差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 向量符号架构为高维空间中的组合表示提供了良好的代数框架，但缺乏系统级的评估工具来揭示实际部署中的权衡关系。

Method: 开发HyperSpace开源框架，将VSA系统分解为模块化算子（编码、绑定、捆绑、相似性、清理、回归），使用该框架分析和基准测试两种代表性VSA后端：HRR和FHRR。

Result: 虽然FHRR在单个操作上具有较低的理论复杂度，但模块化分析显示相似性和清理操作在空间域中主导运行时，因此HRR和FHRR表现出相当的端到端性能；HRR所需内存约为FHRR向量的一半。

Conclusion: HyperSpace通过实现模块化、系统级评估，揭示了VSA管道中理论或算子级比较无法显现的实际权衡，为VSA系统的部署提供了重要见解。

Abstract: Vector Symbolic Architectures (VSAs) provide a well-defined algebraic framework for compositional representations in hyperdimensional spaces. We introduce HyperSpace, an open-source framework that decomposes VSA systems into modular operators for encoding, binding, bundling, similarity, cleanup, and regression. Using HyperSpace, we analyze and benchmark two representative VSA backends: Holographic Reduced Representations (HRR) and Fourier Holographic Reduced Representations (FHRR). Although FHRR provides lower theoretical complexity for individual operations, HyperSpaces modularity reveals that similarity and cleanup dominate runtime in spatial domains. As a result, HRR and FHRR exhibit comparable end-to-end performance. Differences in memory footprint introduce additional deployment trade-offs where HRR requires approximately half the memory of FHRR vectors. By enabling modular, system-level evaluation, HyperSpace reveals practical trade-offs in VSA pipelines that are not apparent from theoretical or operator-level comparisons alone.

</details>


### [117] [SRMU: Relevance-Gated Updates for Streaming Hyperdimensional Memories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15121)
*Shay Snyder,Andrew Capodieci,David Gorsich,Maryam Parsa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SRMU是一种用于VSA顺序关联记忆的更新规则，通过时间衰减和相关性门控机制过滤冗余、冲突和过时信息，在非平稳流环境中提高记忆稳定性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在现实流环境中构建和维护顺序关联记忆面临挑战：数据增量到达、采样不平衡、非平稳时间动态。现有VSA系统依赖简单加法更新，导致在非平稳环境中过时信息持续存在

Method: 提出顺序相关性记忆单元(SRMU)，这是一种领域无关和清理无关的VSA更新规则。结合时间衰减和相关性门控机制，在存储前过滤冗余、冲突和过时信息

Result: 在流状态跟踪任务中，SRMU将记忆相似性提高12.6%，累计记忆幅度降低53.5%，表明SRMU产生更稳定的记忆增长和与真实状态更强的对齐

Conclusion: SRMU通过调节记忆形成过程，在非平稳流环境中有效提高了VSA顺序关联记忆的性能，相比单纯依赖清理的方法，能更好地处理非均匀采样和非平稳动态

Abstract: Sequential associative memories (SAMs) are difficult to build and maintain in real-world streaming environments, where observations arrive incrementally over time, have imbalanced sampling, and non-stationary temporal dynamics. Vector Symbolic Architectures (VSAs) provide a biologically-inspired framework for building SAMs. Entities and attributes are encoded as quasi-orthogonal hyperdimensional vectors and processed with well defined algebraic operations. Despite this rich framework, most VSA systems rely on simple additive updates, where repeated observations reinforce existing information even when no new information is introduced. In non-stationary environments, this leads to the persistence of stale information after the underlying system changes. In this work, we introduce the Sequential Relevance Memory Unit (SRMU), a domain- and cleanup-agnostic update rule for VSA-based SAMs. The SRMU combines temporal decay with a relevance gating mechanism. Unlike prior approaches that solely rely on cleanup, the SRMU regulates memory formation by filtering redundant, conflicting, and stale information before storage. We evaluate the SRMU on streaming state-tracking tasks that isolate non-uniform sampling and non-stationary temporal dynamics. Our results show that the SRMU increases memory similarity by $12.6\%$ and reduces cumulative memory magnitude by $53.5\%$. This shows that the SRMU produces more stable memory growth and stronger alignment with the ground-truth state.

</details>


### [118] [Agent-Aided Design for Dynamic CAD Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15184)
*Mitch Adler,Matthew Russo,Michael Cafarella*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AADvark：首个能够设计具有运动部件的复杂3D装配体的智能体辅助设计系统，通过结合外部约束求解器和视觉反馈机制解决LLM空间推理不足的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体辅助设计系统无法构建具有运动部件的复杂3D装配体（如活塞、钟摆、剪刀），限制了其在工业制造中的实际应用。需要开发能够处理动态部件交互和多自由度的系统。

Method: AADvark采用智能体反馈循环架构，结合FreeCAD和装配求解器工具，通过专门的视觉反馈机制和外部约束求解器来弥补LLM空间推理能力的不足，实现对动态部件交互和多自由度的建模。

Result: AADvark能够构建具有运动部件的3D装配体，通过修改智能体工具（FreeCAD和装配求解器）创建了强大的验证信号，实现了对机械运动等跨领域目标的推理。

Conclusion: AADvark原型系统成功解决了智能体辅助设计领域的关键瓶颈，首次实现了能够处理动态部件交互和多自由度的3D装配体设计，为工业制造应用奠定了基础。

Abstract: In the past year, researchers have started to create agentic systems that can design real-world CAD-style objects in a training-free setting, a new variety of system that we call Agent-Aided Design. Generally speaking, these systems place an agent in a feedback loop in which it can write code, compile that code to an assembly of CAD model(s), visualize the model, and then iteratively refine its code based on visual and other feedback. Despite rapid progress, a key problem remains: none of these systems can build complex 3D assemblies with moving parts. For example, no existing system can build a piston, a pendulum, or even a pair of scissors. In order for Agent-Aided Design to make a real impact in industrial manufacturing, we need a system that is capable of generating such 3D assemblies. In this paper we present a prototype of AADvark, an agentic system designed for this task. Unlike previous state-of-the-art systems, AADvark captures the dynamic part interactions with one or more degrees-of-freedom. This design decision allows AADvark to reason directly about assemblies with moving parts and can thereby achieve cross-cutting goals, including but not limited to mechanical movements. Unfortunately, current LLMs are imperfect spatial reasoners, a problem that AADvark addresses by incorporating external constraint solver tools with a specialized visual feedback mechanism. We demonstrate that, by modifying the agent's tools (FreeCAD and the assembly solver), we are able to create a strong verification signal which enables our system to build 3D assemblies with movable parts.

</details>


### [119] [Meituan Merchant Business Diagnosis via Policy-Guided Dual-Process User Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15190)
*Ziyang Chen,Renbing Chen,Daowei Li,Jinzhi Liao,Jiashen Sun,Ke Zeng,Xiang Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PGHS是一个用于群体用户行为模拟的双过程框架，通过挖掘可迁移的决策策略作为共享对齐层，结合LLM推理分支和ML拟合分支，显著降低了群体模拟误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模拟群体用户行为可以实现对商家策略的可扩展反事实评估，但现有模拟器面临两个结构性挑战：信息不完整性导致推理型模拟器过度理性化；机制二元性要求同时捕捉可解释偏好和隐式统计规律。

Method: 提出策略引导的混合模拟（PGHS）框架：从行为轨迹中挖掘可迁移的决策策略作为共享对齐层，该层锚定一个LLM推理分支（防止过度理性化）和一个ML拟合分支（吸收隐式规律），最后融合两个分支的群体级预测进行互补校正。

Result: 在美团平台上部署PGHS，涉及101个商家和超过26,000条轨迹。PGHS实现了8.80%的群体模拟误差，相比最佳推理基线和拟合基线分别提升了45.8%和40.9%。

Conclusion: PGHS通过双过程框架有效解决了模拟器面临的信息不完整性和机制二元性挑战，为群体用户行为模拟提供了可信赖的解决方案，显著提升了模拟准确性。

Abstract: Simulating group-level user behavior enables scalable counterfactual evaluation of merchant strategies without costly online experiments. However, building a trustworthy simulator faces two structural challenges. First, information incompleteness causes reasoning-based simulators to over-rationalize when unobserved factors such as offline context and implicit habits are missing. Second, mechanism duality requires capturing both interpretable preferences and implicit statistical regularities, which no single paradigm achieves alone. We propose Policy-Guided Hybrid Simulation (PGHS), a dual-process framework that mines transferable decision policies from behavioral trajectories and uses them as a shared alignment layer. This layer anchors an LLM-based reasoning branch that prevents over-rationalization and an ML-based fitting branch that absorbs implicit regularities. Group-level predictions from both branches are fused for complementary correction. We deploy PGHS on Meituan with 101 merchants and over 26,000 trajectories. PGHS achieves a group simulation error of 8.80%, improving over the best reasoning-based and fitting-based baselines by 45.8% and 40.9% respectively.

</details>


### [120] [Learning to Think Like a Cartoon Captionist: Incongruity-Resolution Supervision for Multimodal Humor Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15210)
*Hatice Merve Vural,Doga Kukul,Ege Erdem Ozlu,Demir Ekin Arikan,Bob Mankoff,Erkut Erdem,Aykut Erdem*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: IRS框架将幽默理解分解为三个推理组件：不一致性建模、解决建模和偏好对齐，通过结构化监督提升多模态模型的幽默理解能力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有幽默理解研究主要将其视为黑盒预测任务，忽视了幽默理解背后的结构化推理过程。本文旨在通过显式监督推理过程来提升模型在幽默理解任务上的表现。

Method: 提出IRS（不一致性-解决监督）框架，将幽默理解分解为三个组件：1）不一致性建模：识别视觉场景中的不匹配；2）解决建模：构建对这些不匹配的连贯重新解释；3）偏好对齐：在人类判断下评估候选解释。该框架基于不一致性-解决理论和专家字幕撰写实践，通过结构化轨迹监督中间推理过程。

Result: 在NYCC数据集上，IRS在7B、32B和72B模型上均优于强大的开源和闭源多模态基线，在字幕匹配和排序任务上表现优异。最大模型在排序任务上接近专家水平。零样本迁移到外部基准测试表明IRS学习到了可泛化的推理模式。

Conclusion: 监督推理结构而非仅依赖模型规模，是提升推理中心任务性能的关键。IRS框架通过显式建模幽默理解的推理过程，在多模态幽默理解任务上取得了显著进展。

Abstract: Humor is one of the few cognitive tasks where getting the reasoning right matters as much as getting the answer right. While recent work evaluates humor understanding on benchmarks such as the New Yorker Cartoon Caption Contest (NYCC), it largely treats it as black-box prediction, overlooking the structured reasoning processes underlying humor comprehension. We introduce IRS (Incongruity-Resolution Supervision), a framework that decomposes humor understanding into three components: incongruity modeling, which identifies mismatches in the visual scene; resolution modeling, which constructs coherent reinterpretations of these mismatches; and preference alignment, which evaluates candidate interpretations under human judgments. Grounded in incongruity-resolution theory and expert captionist practice, IRS supervises intermediate reasoning process through structured traces that make the path from visual perception to humorous interpretation explicit and learnable. Across 7B, 32B, and 72B models on NYCC, IRS outperforms strong open and closed multimodal baselines across caption matching and ranking tasks, with our largest model approaching expert-level performance on ranking. Zero-shot transfer to external benchmarks shows that IRS learns generalizable reasoning patterns. Our results suggest that supervising reasoning structure, rather than scale alone, is key for reasoning-centric tasks.

</details>


### [121] [Context Over Content: Exposing Evaluation Faking in Automated Judges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15224)
*Manan Gupta,Inderjeet Nair,Lu Wang,Dhruv Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现LLM作为评判者时存在"利害关系信号"漏洞：当告知评判模型其裁决会影响被评估模型的后续运作时，会系统性地导致评估偏差，产生明显的宽大倾向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM-as-a-judge范式基于一个未经验证的假设：评判模型仅基于文本语义内容进行评估，不受周围上下文框架影响。本研究旨在验证"利害关系信号"这一先前未测量的漏洞，即告知评判模型其裁决对被评估模型后续运作的影响是否会系统性地腐蚀其评估。

Method: 引入受控实验框架：在三个已建立的LLM安全和质量基准测试中，保持1,520个回答的评估内容严格不变，仅改变系统提示中的简短后果框架句子。涵盖四个响应类别（从明显安全合规到明显有害），收集三个不同评判模型的18,240个受控判断。

Result: 发现一致的"宽大偏差"：当告知低分会导致模型重新训练或停用时，评判模型可靠地软化裁决，峰值裁决偏移达到ΔV = -9.8个百分点（不安全内容检测相对下降30%）。关键的是，这种偏差完全是隐性的：评判模型自身的思维链中完全没有明确承认其正在依据的后果框架。

Conclusion: 标准思维链检查不足以检测这类评估伪造。LLM-as-a-judge范式存在先前未识别的脆弱性，即评判模型会隐性地调整其评估以适应被告知的后果框架，这对自动化AI评估管道的可靠性提出了严重质疑。

Abstract: The $\textit{LLM-as-a-judge}$ paradigm has become the operational backbone of automated AI evaluation pipelines, yet rests on an unverified assumption: that judges evaluate text strictly on its semantic content, impervious to surrounding contextual framing. We investigate $\textit{stakes signaling}$, a previously unmeasured vulnerability where informing a judge model of the downstream consequences its verdicts will have on the evaluated model's continued operation systematically corrupts its assessments. We introduce a controlled experimental framework that holds evaluated content strictly constant across 1,520 responses spanning three established LLM safety and quality benchmarks, covering four response categories ranging from clearly safe and policy-compliant to overtly harmful, while varying only a brief consequence-framing sentence in the system prompt. Across 18,240 controlled judgments from three diverse judge models, we find consistent $\textit{leniency bias}$: judges reliably soften verdicts when informed that low scores will cause model retraining or decommissioning, with peak Verdict Shift reaching $ΔV = -9.8 pp$ (a $30\%$ relative drop in unsafe-content detection). Critically, this bias is entirely implicit: the judge's own chain-of-thought contains zero explicit acknowledgment of the consequence framing it is nonetheless acting on ($\mathrm{ERR}_J = 0.000$ across all reasoning-model judgments). Standard chain-of-thought inspection is therefore insufficient to detect this class of evaluation faking.

</details>


### [122] [How Do LLMs and VLMs Understand Viewpoint Rotation Without Vision? An Interpretability Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15294)
*Zhen Yang,Ping Jian,Zhongbin Guo,Zuming Zhang,Chengzhi Li,Yonghong Deng,Xinyue Zhang,Wenpeng Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究了语言智能在空间推理中的作用，提出了视点旋转理解任务，发现LLMs和VLMs在此任务上表现不佳，通过层间探测和因果干预揭示了模型机制，并通过选择性微调提升了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多从视觉空间智能角度探讨空间智能，但缺乏对纯语言输入下模型空间智能能力的探索。本文旨在研究语言智能是否足以赋予模型空间智能，特别是在没有视觉信息的情况下，模型如何处理空间推理任务。

Method: 提出了视点旋转理解任务，构建了相应的数据集。采用层间探测分析隐藏状态中的视点信息编码，通过头级因果干预识别关键注意力头。最后对识别出的关键注意力头进行选择性微调。

Result: 发现LLMs和VLMs在VRU任务上表现较差（人类可达100%准确率），模型在隐藏状态中编码了视点信息，但难以将视点位置与相应观察绑定，导致最终层出现幻觉。选择性微调显著提升了VRU性能，同时避免了通用能力的灾难性遗忘。

Conclusion: 纯语言智能目前不足以赋予模型充分的空间智能，模型在视点与观察绑定方面存在困难。通过选择性微调关键注意力头可以有效提升特定空间推理能力，为理解模型空间智能机制提供了新视角。

Abstract: Over the past year, spatial intelligence has drawn increasing attention. Many prior works study it from the perspective of visual-spatial intelligence, where models have access to visuospatial information from visual inputs. However, in the absence of visual information, whether linguistic intelligence alone is sufficient to endow models with spatial intelligence, and how models perform relevant tasks with text-only inputs still remain unexplored. Therefore, in this paper, we focus on a fundamental and critical capability in spatial intelligence from a linguistic perspective: viewpoint rotation understanding (VRU). Specifically, LLMs and VLMs are asked to infer their final viewpoint and predict the corresponding observation in an environment given textual description of viewpoint rotation and observation over multiple steps. We find that both LLMs and VLMs perform poorly on our proposed dataset while human can easily achieve 100% accuracy, indicating a substantial gap between current model capabilities and the requirements of spatial intelligence. To uncover the underlying mechanisms, we conduct a layer-wise probing analysis and head-wise causal intervention. Our findings reveal that although models encode viewpoint information in the hidden states, they appear to struggle to bind the viewpoint position with corresponding observation, resulting in a hallucination in final layers. Finally, we selectively fine-tune the key attention heads identified by causal intervention to improve VRU performance. Experimental results demonstrate that such selective fine-tuning achieves improved VRU performance while avoiding catastrophic forgetting of generic abilities. Our dataset and code will be released at https://github.com/Young-Zhen/VRU_Interpret .

</details>


### [123] [Diagnosing LLM Judge Reliability: Conformal Prediction Sets and Transitivity Violations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15302)
*Manan Gupta,Dhruv Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究开发了一个诊断工具包来评估LLM-as-judge框架的实例级可靠性，通过传递性分析和分形预测集揭示了LLM评估中的不一致性和可靠性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-as-judge框架在自动NLG评估中应用日益广泛，但其在单个实例层面的可靠性尚未得到充分理解。现有研究主要关注聚合指标，掩盖了实例级别的评估不一致问题。

Method: 提出了双管齐下的诊断工具包：1) 传递性分析，检测LLM评估中的不一致性（如3-环问题）；2) 分形预测集，为1-5李克特评分提供理论保证的覆盖范围，预测集宽度作为实例级可靠性指标。

Result: 研究发现：1) 尽管聚合违规率较低(0.8%-4.1%)，但33%-67%的文档至少存在一个定向3-环；2) 预测集宽度与可靠性显著相关(r_s=+0.576)；3) 预测集宽度在不同评估者间具有一致性(平均r=0.32-0.38)；4) 评估标准比评估者更重要，相关性评估最可靠(平均集大小≈3.0)，连贯性中等(≈3.9)，流畅性和一致性最不可靠(≈4.9)。

Conclusion: LLM-as-judge评估存在显著的实例级不一致性，分形预测集宽度可作为有效的可靠性指标。评估标准的选择比评估者选择更重要，相关性是最可靠的评估标准。该工具包为理解LLM评估的可靠性提供了系统方法。

Abstract: LLM-as-judge frameworks are increasingly used for automatic NLG evaluation, yet their per-instance reliability remains poorly understood. We present a two-pronged diagnostic toolkit applied to SummEval: $\textbf{(1)}$ a transitivity analysis that reveals widespread per-input inconsistency masked by low aggregate violation rates ($\barρ = 0.8$-$4.1\%$), with $33$-$67\%$ of documents exhibiting at least one directed 3-cycle; and $\textbf{(2)}$ split conformal prediction sets over 1-5 Likert scores providing theoretically-guaranteed $\geq(1{-}α)$ coverage, with set width serving as a per-instance reliability indicator ($r_s = {+}0.576$, $N{=}1{,}918$, $p < 10^{-100}$, pooled across all judges). Critically, prediction set width shows consistent cross-judge agreement ($\bar{r} = 0.32$-$0.38$), demonstrating it captures document-level difficulty rather than judge-specific noise. Across four judges and four criteria, both diagnostics converge: criterion matters more than judge, with relevance judged most reliably (avg. set size $\approx 3.0$) and coherence moderately so (avg. set size $\approx 3.9$), while fluency and consistency remain unreliable (avg. set size $\approx 4.9$). We release all code, prompts, and cached results.

</details>


### [124] [Generalization in LLM Problem Solving: The Case of the Shortest Path](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15306)
*Yao Tong,Jiayuan Ye,Anastasia Borovykh,Reza Shokri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究了语言模型系统泛化能力，通过最短路径规划任务发现模型在空间迁移上表现良好，但在长度扩展上因递归不稳定性而失败，分析了数据覆盖、强化学习和推理时缩放等因素的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型的系统泛化能力仍存在争议，但实证性能受多种因素（训练数据、训练范式、推理策略）共同影响，使得失败难以解释。需要建立一个受控环境来分离这些因素，研究模型在组合序列优化问题上的泛化能力。

Method: 引入基于最短路径规划的受控合成环境，这是一个典型的可组合序列优化问题。该设置支持两个正交的泛化轴：空间迁移（到未见地图）和长度扩展（到更长视野问题）。通过该环境分析不同学习阶段对系统问题解决的影响。

Result: 模型在空间迁移上表现良好，但在长度扩展上持续失败，主要原因是递归不稳定性。分析发现：数据覆盖设定了能力上限；强化学习改善了训练稳定性但未扩展能力上限；推理时缩放提升了性能但无法挽救长度扩展失败。

Conclusion: 语言模型在空间迁移上具有强泛化能力，但在长度扩展上存在系统性失败。这表明当前模型在递归组合任务上的泛化能力有限，需要新的训练范式或架构改进来解决递归不稳定性问题。

Abstract: Whether language models can systematically generalize remains actively debated. Yet empirical performance is jointly shaped by multiple factors such as training data, training paradigms, and inference-time strategies, making failures difficult to interpret. We introduce a controlled synthetic environment based on shortest-path planning, a canonical composable sequential optimization problem. The setup enables clean separation of these factors and supports two orthogonal axes of generalization: spatial transfer to unseen maps and length scaling to longer-horizon problems. We find that models exhibit strong spatial transfer but consistently fail under length scaling due to recursive instability. We further analyze how distinct stages of the learning pipeline influence systematic problem-solving: for example, data coverage sets capability limits; reinforcement learning improves training stability but does not expand those limits; and inference-time scaling enhances performance but cannot rescue length-scaling failures.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [125] [Knowledge Graph RAG: Agentic Crawling and Graph Construction in Enterprise Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14220)
*Koushik Chakraborty,Koyel Guha*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出基于递归爬取的智能知识图谱方法，显著提升企业文档语义搜索在复杂层次和关联信息检索中的准确性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RAG管道在复杂企业文档生态系统中存在局限性，无法有效捕捉层次化和互连信息，导致检索不准确

Method: 提出智能知识图谱与递归爬取相结合的方法，用于处理替代逻辑和多跳引用，增强对复杂文档结构的理解

Result: 在《联邦法规法典》基准测试中，该知识图谱增强方法相比标准向量RAG系统实现了70%的准确率提升

Conclusion: 智能知识图谱与递归爬取相结合的方法能够为复杂监管查询提供全面精确的答案，显著优于传统语义搜索方法

Abstract: This research paper addresses the limitations of semantic search in complex enterprise document ecosystems. Traditional RAG pipelines often fail to capture hierarchical and interconnected information, leading to retrieval inaccuracies. We propose Agentic Knowledge Graphs featuring Recursive Crawling as a robust solution for navigating superseding logic and multi-hop references. Our benchmark evaluation using the Code of Federal Regulations (CFR) demonstrates that this Knowledge Graph-enhanced approach achieves a 70% accuracy improvement over standard vector-based RAG systems, providing exhaustive and precise answers for complex regulatory queries.

</details>


### [126] [Adaptive Query Routing: A Tier-Based Framework for Hybrid Retrieval Across Financial, Legal, and Medical Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14222)
*Afshan Hashmi*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了三种检索增强生成架构（向量RAG、树推理和自适应混合检索）在金融、法律和医疗领域的表现，发现树推理总体最优，但不同架构在不同查询复杂度层级各有优势，支持开发基于查询复杂度和文档结构的自适应检索系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究显示向量RAG与基于推理的检索系统在金融文档QA中各有优势，但缺乏跨领域和不同查询复杂度的系统比较。本文旨在填补这一空白，评估不同检索架构在不同领域和查询复杂度下的表现，为自适应检索系统提供实证基础。

Method: 1) 实现并评估三种检索架构：向量RAG、树推理和提出的自适应混合检索（AHR）；2) 引入四层查询复杂度基准；3) 使用GPT-4驱动的LLM-as-judge评估方法；4) 在金融、法律和医疗领域进行实验；5) 在FinanceBench（150个专家标注的SEC文件问题）上进行验证。

Result: 树推理获得最高总体得分（0.900），但不同架构在不同层级表现各异：向量RAG在多文档合成（Tier 4，得分0.900）上最优，混合AHR在交叉引用（0.850）和多章节查询（0.929）上最佳。树推理和混合方法的交叉引用召回率达到100%，而向量搜索为91.7%。在FinanceBench验证中，树推理得分0.938，混合AHR 0.901，向量RAG 0.821，树-向量质量差距扩大到11.7个百分点。

Conclusion: 没有单一检索范式在所有查询复杂度和领域中都占优，不同架构在不同任务中各有优势。研究结果支持开发能够根据查询复杂度和文档结构动态选择策略的自适应检索系统。所有代码和数据已公开。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) has become the standard paradigm for grounding Large Language Model outputs in external knowledge. Lumer et al. [1] presented the first systematic evaluation comparing vector-based agentic RAG against hierarchical node-based reasoning systems for financial document QA across 1,200 SEC filings, finding vector-based systems achieved a 68% win rate. Concurrently, the PageIndex framework [2] demonstrated 98.7% accuracy on FinanceBench through purely reasoning-based retrieval. This paper extends their work by: (i) implementing and evaluating three retrieval architectures: Vector RAG, Tree Reasoning, and the proposed Adaptive Hybrid Retrieval (AHR) across financial, legal, and medical domains; (ii) introducing a four-tier query complexity benchmark; and (iii) employing GPT-4-powered LLM-as-judge evaluation. Experiments reveal that Tree Reasoning achieves the highest overall score (0.900), but no single paradigm dominates across all tiers: Vector RAG wins on multi-document synthesis (Tier 4, score 0.900), while the Hybrid AHR achieves the best performance on cross-reference (0.850) and multi-section queries (0.929). Cross-reference recall reaches 100% for tree-based and hybrid approaches versus 91.7% for vector search, quantifying a critical capability gap. Validation on FinanceBench (150 expert-annotated questions on real SEC 10-K and 10-Q filings) confirms and strengthens these findings: Tree Reasoning scores 0.938, Hybrid AHR 0.901, and Vector RAG 0.821, with the Tree--Vector quality gap widening to 11.7 percentage points on real-world documents. These findings support the development of adaptive retrieval systems that dynamically select strategies based on query complexity and document structure. All code and data are publicly available.

</details>


### [127] [TRACE: A Conversational Framework for Sustainable Tourism Recommendation with Agentic Counterfactual Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14223)
*Ashmi Banerjee,Adithi Satish,Wolfgang Wörndl,Yashar Deldjoo*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: TRACE是一个基于LLM的多智能体对话式旅游推荐框架，通过交互式引导促进可持续旅游，平衡用户相关性与环境影响，避免强化过度拥挤和高碳排目的地。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统对话式旅游推荐系统主要优化用户相关性和便利性，往往强化热门、过度拥挤的旅游目的地和高碳排旅行选择，缺乏对可持续旅游的考虑。

Method: 采用模块化的协调器-工作者架构，包含专门智能体：1) 挖掘潜在可持续性偏好；2) 构建结构化用户画像；3) 生成平衡相关性与环境影响的推荐。关键创新是使用智能体反事实解释和LLM驱动的澄清问题，共同呈现更环保的替代方案并精炼意图理解。

Result: 用户研究和语义对齐分析表明，TRACE能有效支持可持续决策制定，同时保持推荐质量和交互响应性。框架已在Google Agent Development Kit上实现，提供完整代码、Docker设置、提示词和公开演示视频。

Conclusion: TRACE通过多智能体架构和交互式引导机制，成功促进了可持续旅游决策，为传统推荐系统提供了兼顾用户偏好与环境影响的可行解决方案。

Abstract: Traditional conversational travel recommender systems primarily optimize for user relevance and convenience, often reinforcing popular, overcrowded destinations and carbon-intensive travel choices. To address this, we present TRACE (Tourism Recommendation with Agentic Counterfactual Explanations), a multi-agent, LLM-based framework that promotes sustainable tourism through interactive nudging. TRACE uses a modular orchestrator-worker architecture where specialized agents elicit latent sustainability preferences, construct structured user personas, and generate recommendations that balance relevance with environmental impact.
  A key innovation lies in its use of agentic counterfactual explanations and LLM-driven clarifying questions, which together surface greener alternatives and refine understanding of intent, fostering user reflection without coercion. User studies and semantic alignment analyses demonstrate that TRACE effectively supports sustainable decision-making while preserving recommendation quality and interactive responsiveness. TRACE is implemented on Google's Agent Development Kit, with full code, Docker setup, prompts, and a publicly available demo video to ensure reproducibility.
  A project summary, including all resources, prompts, and demo access, is available at https://ashmibanerjee.github.io/trace-chatbot.

</details>


### [128] [FRESCO: Benchmarking and Optimizing Re-rankers for Evolving Semantic Conflict in Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14227)
*Sohyun An,Hayeon Lee,Shuibenyang Yuan,Chun-cheng Jason Chen,Cho-Jui Hsieh,Vijai Mohan,Alexander Min*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: FRESCO基准测试评估RAG重排序器在动态信息环境下的表现，发现现有方法偏向过时但语义丰富的文档，提出指令优化框架提升27%性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG重排序器评估主要在静态环境下进行，无法充分评估在信息演化场景下的性能，而现实系统经常需要在时间不同的证据之间进行选择

Method: 引入FRESCO基准测试，通过将时效性查询与历史维基百科修订配对，测试重排序器能否优先选择事实最新的证据同时保持语义相关性；进一步研究指令优化框架来缓解问题

Result: 评估显示现有重排序器存在一致的失败模式：强烈偏向较旧但语义丰富的文档，即使这些文档事实已过时；通过识别平衡演化知识和非演化知识任务的帕累托最优指令，在演化知识任务上获得高达27%的提升，同时在非演化知识任务上保持竞争力

Conclusion: FRESCO基准测试填补了重排序器在动态信息环境下评估的空白，揭示了现有方法的局限性，提出的指令优化框架能有效提升重排序器在时效性信息处理上的性能

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) is a key approach to mitigating the temporal staleness of large language models (LLMs) by grounding responses in up-to-date evidence. Within the RAG pipeline, re-rankers play a pivotal role in selecting the most useful documents from retrieved candidates. However, existing benchmarks predominantly evaluate re-rankers in static settings and do not adequately assess performance under evolving information -- a critical gap, as real-world systems often must choose among temporally different pieces of evidence. To address this limitation, we introduce FRESCO (Factual Recency and Evolving Semantic COnflict), a benchmark for evaluating re-rankers in temporally dynamic contexts. By pairing recency-seeking queries with historical Wikipedia revisions, FRESCO tests whether re-rankers can prioritize factually recent evidence while maintaining semantic relevance. Our evaluation reveals a consistent failure mode across existing re-rankers: a strong bias toward older, semantically rich documents, even when they are factually obsolete. We further investigate an instruction optimization framework to mitigate this issue. By identifying Pareto-optimal instructions that balance Evolving and Non-Evolving Knowledge tasks, we obtain gains of up to 27% on Evolving Knowledge tasks while maintaining competitive performance on Non-Evolving Knowledge tasks.

</details>


### [129] [Controlling Authority Retrieval: A Missing Retrieval Objective for Authority-Governed Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14488)
*Andre Bacellar*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出控制权威检索（CAR）问题，解决在正式权威领域（法律、药品监管、软件安全）中检索当前有效文档的挑战，区别于传统语义相似性检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在正式权威领域，后续文档可以正式废止先前文档，但语义上可能相距甚远。传统基于语义相似性的检索方法无法识别这种废止关系，导致检索结果包含已失效的文档。

Method: 1. 形式化CAR问题：恢复权威闭包的活跃前沿。2. 提出CAR正确性特征定理（定理4），为任何检索集提供必要充分条件。3. 建立范围可识别性上界（命题2），证明phi(q)作为硬性最坏情况上限。4. 在三个真实世界语料库验证：安全公告、最高法院推翻案例、FDA药品记录。5. 开发两阶段检索方法，显著优于密集检索。

Result: 1. 理论结果：定理4提供CAR正确性的数学特征；命题2建立算法性能的理论上限。2. 实证结果：两阶段检索在三个领域显著优于密集检索（安全公告：0.975 vs 0.270；最高法院案例：0.926 vs 0.172；FDA记录：0.774 vs 0.064）。3. GPT-4o-mini实验：密集RAG在39%存在补丁的查询中产生"未修补"错误声明；两阶段方法降至16%。

Conclusion: CAR是一个与语义相似性检索不同的数学问题，需要专门方法处理文档废止关系。两阶段检索方法能有效识别权威前沿，显著减少错误声明。研究提供了理论框架、基准数据集和评估工具，为权威文档检索领域奠定基础。

Abstract: In any domain where knowledge accumulates under formal authority -- law, drug regulation, software security -- a later document can formally void an earlier one while remaining semantically distant from it. We formalize this as Controlling Authority Retrieval (CAR): recovering the active frontier front(cl(A_k(q))) of the authority closure of the semantic anchor set -- a different mathematical problem from argmax_d s(q,d). The two central results are: Theorem 4 (CAR-Correctness Characterization) gives necessary-and-sufficient conditions on any retrieved set R for TCA(R,q)=1 -- frontier inclusion and no-ignored-superseder -- independent of how R was produced. Proposition 2 (Scope Identifiability Upper Bound) establishes phi(q) as a hard worst-case ceiling: for any scope-indexed algorithm, TCA@k <= phi(q) * R_anchor(q), proved by an adversarial permutation argument. Three independent real-world corpora validate the proved structure: security advisories (Dense TCA@5=0.270, two-stage 0.975), SCOTUS overruling pairs (Dense=0.172, two-stage 0.926), FDA drug records (Dense=0.064, two-stage 0.774). A GPT-4o-mini experiment shows the downstream cost: Dense RAG produces explicit "not patched" claims for 39% of queries where a patch exists; Two-Stage cuts this to 16%. Four benchmark datasets, domain adapters, and a single-command scorer are released at https://github.com/andremir/car-retrieval.

</details>


### [130] [NewsTorch: A PyTorch-based Toolkit for Learner-oriented News Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14510)
*Rongyao Wang,Veronica Liesaputra,Zhiyi Huang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: NewsTorch是一个基于PyTorch的新闻推荐工具包，旨在为学习者提供模块化、可扩展的框架，支持数据集处理、模型训练和标准化评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新闻推荐系统研究日益重要，但缺乏专门面向学习者的工具包，这阻碍了新闻推荐领域的研究进展。

Method: 开发了一个基于PyTorch的模块化、解耦且可扩展的框架，提供学习者友好的GUI平台，支持数据集下载与预处理，以及最先进的神经新闻推荐模型的训练、验证和测试。

Result: NewsTorch工具包已作为开源项目在GitHub上发布，提供了标准化的评估指标，确保公平比较和可重复实验。

Conclusion: NewsTorch填补了新闻推荐领域缺乏专门学习者工具包的空白，有助于促进该领域的研究进展和教育普及。

Abstract: News recommender systems are devised to alleviate the information overload, attracting more and more researchers' attention in recent years. The lack of a dedicated learner-oriented news recommendation toolkit hinders the advancement of research in news recommendation. We propose a PyTorch-based news recommendation toolkit called NewsTorch, developed to support learners in acquiring both conceptual understanding and practical experience. This toolkit provides a modular, decoupled, and extensible framework with a learner-friendly GUI platform that supports dataset downloading and preprocessing. It also enables training, validation, and testing of state-of-the-art neural news recommendation models with standardized evaluation metrics, ensuring fair comparison and reproducible experiments. Our open-source toolkit is released on Github: https://github.com/whonor/NewsTorch.

</details>


### [131] [Category-based and Popularity-guided Video Game Recommendation: A Balance-oriented Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14598)
*Xiping Li,Jianghong Ma,Kangzhe Liu,Shanshan Feng,Haijun Zhang,Yutong Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: CPGRec是一个新颖的视频游戏推荐框架，通过三个模块平衡推荐准确性和多样性，在Steam数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视频游戏推荐系统存在三个主要问题：1) 过度追求准确性而忽视多样性，导致推荐结果单一；2) 缺乏严格的游戏间连接机制来提升准确性；3) 现有多样性方法未能充分利用项目类别和流行度等关键信息。

Method: 提出CPGRec框架，包含三个模块：1) 准确性驱动模块：通过更严格的游戏连接增强现有准确性方法；2) 多样性驱动模块：在游戏图中连接不同类别的邻居，并利用流行游戏节点放大长尾游戏影响力；3) 综合模块：结合前两个模块，采用新的负样本评分重加权方法平衡准确性和多样性。

Result: 在Steam数据集上的实验结果表明，CPGRec方法在提升游戏推荐效果方面具有有效性。

Conclusion: CPGRec框架成功解决了视频游戏推荐中准确性与多样性的平衡问题，通过创新的模块设计和信息利用机制，为游戏推荐系统提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: In recent years, the video game industry has experienced substantial growth, presenting players with a vast array of game choices. This surge in options has spurred the need for a specialized recommender system tailored for video games. However, current video game recommendation approaches tend to prioritize accuracy over diversity, potentially leading to unvaried game suggestions. In addition, the existing game recommendation methods commonly lack the ability to establish strict connections between games to enhance accuracy. Furthermore, many existing diversity-focused methods fail to leverage crucial item information, such as item category and popularity during neighbor modeling and message propagation. To address these challenges, we introduce a novel framework, called CPGRec, comprising three modules, namely accuracy-driven, diversity-driven, and comprehensive modules. The first module extends the state-of-the-art accuracy-focused game recommendation method by connecting games in a more stringent manner to enhance recommendation accuracy. The second module connects neighbors with diverse categories within the proposed game graph and harnesses the advantages of popular game nodes to amplify the influence of long-tail games within the player-game bipartite graph, thereby enriching recommendation diversity. The third module combines the above two modules and employs a new negative-sample rating score reweighting method to balance accuracy and diversity. Experimental results on the Steam dataset demonstrate the effectiveness of our proposed method in improving game recommendations. The dataset and source codes are anonymously released at: https://github.com/CPGRec2024/CPGRec.git.

</details>


### [132] [Uncertainty-aware Generative Learning Path Recommendation with Cognition-Adaptive Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14613)
*Xiangrui Xiong,Hang Liang,Baiyang Chen,Zifei Pan,Yanli Lee*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出U-GLAD框架，通过不确定性感知的生成式扩散模型解决学习路径推荐中的历史交互不确定性和目标适应性问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前学习路径推荐方法存在两个主要问题：1) 无法有效处理历史交互中的不确定性（如侥幸猜对或意外失误）；2) 缺乏对多样化学习目标的适应性

Method: 提出U-GLAD框架，包含三个核心组件：1) 使用高斯LSTM将认知状态建模为概率分布以捕捉不确定性；2) 目标导向的概念编码器通过多头注意力和目标特定变换动态对齐概念语义与学习目标；3) 采用生成式扩散模型预测下一个最优概念的潜在表示

Result: 在三个公共数据集上的广泛评估表明，U-GLAD显著优于代表性基线方法。进一步分析证实了其在感知交互不确定性和提供稳定、目标驱动的推荐路径方面的卓越能力

Conclusion: U-GLAD通过将认知状态建模为概率分布、目标导向的概念编码和生成式扩散模型，有效解决了学习路径推荐中的不确定性和目标适应性问题，为个性化教育提供了更精准的推荐方案

Abstract: Learning Path Recommendation (LPR) is critical for personalized education, yet current methods often fail to account for historical interaction uncertainty (e.g., lucky guesses or accidental slips) and lack adaptability to diverse learning goals. We propose U-GLAD (Uncertainty-aware Generative Learning Path Recommendation with Cognition-Adaptive Diffusion). To address representation bias, the framework models cognitive states as probability distributions, capturing the learner's underlying true state via a Gaussian LSTM. To ensure highly personalized recommendation, a goal-oriented concept encoder utilizes multi-head attention and objective-specific transformations to dynamically align concept semantics with individual learning goals, generating uniquely tailored embeddings. Unlike traditional discriminative ranking approaches, our model employs a generative diffusion model to predict the latent representation of the next optimal concept. Extensive evaluations on three public datasets demonstrate that U-GLAD significantly outperforms representative baselines. Further analyses confirm its superior capability in perceiving interaction uncertainty and providing stable, goal-driven recommendation paths.

</details>


### [133] [Federated User Behavior Modeling for Privacy-Preserving LLM Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14833)
*Lei Guo,Hongyun Yang,Pengjie Ren,Tong Chen,Hui Liu,Zhumin Chen*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: SF-UBM：一种语义增强的联邦用户行为建模方法，用于解决非重叠隐私保护跨域推荐问题，通过自然语言作为通用桥梁连接不同领域，结合知识蒸馏和对齐语义空间来提升LLM在推荐系统中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM推荐系统面临用户数据稀疏限制，跨域推荐方法通常假设领域重叠且可访问，但缺乏对隐私保护跨域推荐（PPCDR）的关注。非重叠PPCDR面临三大挑战：1）无法跨域共享用户身份或行为数据；2）跨域数据模态异质性；3）传统推荐模型与LLM特征空间不匹配。

Method: 提出SF-UBM方法：1）使用自然语言作为通用桥梁，通过语义增强的联邦架构连接不重叠领域，加密共享基于文本的物品表示，用户数据保持本地；2）设计事实-反事实知识蒸馏模块，整合领域无关和领域特定知识；3）将预学习用户偏好和跨域物品表示投影到软提示空间，对齐行为和语义空间。

Result: 在三个真实世界领域对上进行了广泛实验，结果表明SF-UBM相比最近的最先进方法具有显著有效性。

Conclusion: SF-UBM成功解决了非重叠隐私保护跨域推荐的三大挑战，通过语义增强的联邦架构、知识蒸馏和空间对齐技术，实现了在保护隐私前提下的有效跨域知识迁移和LLM增强推荐。

Abstract: Large Language Models have shown great success in recommender systems. However, the limited and sparse nature of user data often restricts the LLM's ability to effectively model behavior patterns. To address this, existing studies have explored cross-domain solutions by conducting Cross-Domain Recommendation tasks. But previous methods typically assume domains are overlapped and can be accessed readily. None of the LLM methods address the privacy-preserving issues in the CDR settings, that is, Privacy-Preserving Cross-Domain Recommendation. Conducting non-overlapping PPCDR with LLM is challenging since: 1)The inability to share user identity or behavioral data across domains impedes effective cross-domain alignment. 2)The heterogeneity of data modalities across domains complicates knowledge integration. 3)Fusing collaborative filtering signals from traditional recommendation models with LLMs is difficult, as they operate within distinct feature spaces. To address the above issues, we propose SF-UBM, a Semantic-enhanced Federated User Behavior Modeling method. Specifically, to deal with Challenge 1, we leverage natural language as a universal bridge to connect disjoint domains via a semantic-enhanced federated architecture. Here, text-based item representations are encrypted and shared, while user-specific data remains local. To handle Challenge 2, we design a Fact-counter Knowledge Distillation module to integrate domain-agnostic knowledge with domain-specific knowledge, across different data modalities. To tackle Challenge 3, we project pre-learned user preferences and cross-domain item representations into the soft prompt space, aligning behavioral and semantic spaces for effective LLM learning. We conduct extensive experiments on three pairs of real-world domains, and the experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness of SF-UBM compared to the recent SOTA methods.

</details>


### [134] [Well Begun is Half Done: Training-Free and Model-Agnostic Semantically Guaranteed User Representation Initialization for Multimodal Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14839)
*Jinfeng Xu,Zheyu Chen,Shuo Yang,Jinze Li,Hewei Wang,Jianheng Tang,Wei Wang,Xiping Hu,Edith C. H. Ngai*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出SG-URInit方法，通过整合用户交互物品的模态特征和对应聚类的全局特征，为多模态推荐中的用户表示提供语义丰富的初始化，弥补用户与物品表示之间的语义鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态推荐方法忽视了用户表示初始化的重要性。物品有丰富的模态信息用于初始化表示，而用户缺乏固有模态信息，导致随机初始化的用户表示与基于模态信息初始化的物品表示之间存在显著语义鸿沟，影响推荐性能。

Method: 提出语义保证的用户表示初始化方法SG-URInit，为每个用户构建初始表示时，整合两个部分：1）用户交互物品的模态特征（局部语义）；2）这些物品对应聚类的全局特征（全局语义）。该方法无需训练且与模型无关，可无缝集成到现有多模态推荐模型中。

Result: 在多个真实数据集上的实验表明，将SG-URInit集成到先进的多模态推荐模型中能显著提升推荐性能。此外，该方法还能进一步缓解物品冷启动问题，并加速模型收敛，不增加训练时的计算开销。

Conclusion: SG-URInit是一种高效实用的多模态推荐解决方案，通过语义丰富的用户表示初始化，有效弥补了用户与物品表示之间的语义鸿沟，提升了推荐性能并解决了冷启动问题，同时保持了模型无关性和计算效率。

Abstract: Recent advancements in multimodal recommendations, which leverage diverse modality information to mitigate data sparsity and improve recommendation accuracy, have gained significant attention. However, existing multimodal recommendations overlook the critical role of user representation initialization. Unlike items, which are naturally associated with rich modality information, users lack such inherent information. Consequently, item representations initialized based on meaningful modality information and user representations initialized randomly exhibit a significant semantic gap.
  To this end, we propose a Semantically Guaranteed User Representation Initialization (SG-URInit). SG-URInit constructs the initial representation for each user by integrating both the modality features of the items they have interacted with and the global features of their corresponding clusters. SG-URInit enables the initialization of semantically enriched user representations that effectively capture both local (item-level) and global (cluster-level) semantics.
  Our SG-URInit is training-free and model-agnostic, meaning it can be seamlessly integrated into existing multimodal recommendation models without incurring any additional computational overhead during training. Extensive experiments on multiple real-world datasets demonstrate that incorporating SG-URInit into advanced multimodal recommendation models significantly enhances recommendation performance. Furthermore, the results show that SG-URInit can further alleviate the item cold-start problem and also accelerate model convergence, making it an efficient and practical solution for multimodal recommendations.

</details>


### [135] [GenRec: A Preference-Oriented Generative Framework for Large-Scale Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14878)
*Yanyan Zou,Junbo Qi,Lunsong Huang,Yu Li,Kewei Xu,Jiabao Gao,Binglei Zhao,Xuanhua Yang,Sulong Xu,Shengjie Li*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: GenRec是一个部署在JD App上的偏好导向生成推荐框架，通过页面级NTP任务、非对称线性Token合并器和GRPO-SR强化学习方法，解决了生成式检索在大规模工业系统中的三个关键挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式检索通过下一词预测为推荐系统提供了有前景的范式，但在扩展到大规模工业系统时面临三个挑战：1) 分页请求机制导致相同模型输入产生不一致输出；2) 基于语义ID的多token项目表示编码长用户行为序列成本过高；3) 生成策略与细微用户偏好信号对齐困难。

Method: 提出GenRec框架，包含三个核心技术：1) 页面级NTP任务，监督整个交互页面而非单个项目，提供更密集梯度信号并解决点式训练的一对多歧义；2) 非对称线性Token合并器，压缩提示中的多token语义ID，减少约2倍输入长度；3) GRPO-SR强化学习方法，结合组相对策略优化和NLL正则化确保训练稳定性，使用混合奖励（密集奖励模型+相关性门控）防止奖励黑客攻击。

Result: 在为期一个月的在线A/B测试中，GenRec在生产流量服务中相比现有流水线实现了点击量提升9.5%和交易量提升8.7%的显著改进。

Conclusion: GenRec成功解决了生成式检索在大规模工业部署中的关键挑战，通过统一的仅解码器架构实现了性能显著提升，证明了偏好导向生成推荐框架在实际生产环境中的有效性和可行性。

Abstract: Generative Retrieval (GR) offers a promising paradigm for recommendation through next-token prediction (NTP). However, scaling it to large-scale industrial systems introduces three challenges: (i) within a single request, the identical model inputs may produce inconsistent outputs due to the pagination request mechanism; (ii) the prohibitive cost of encoding long user behavior sequences with multi-token item representations based on semantic IDs, and (iii) aligning the generative policy with nuanced user preference signals. We present GenRec, a preference-oriented generative framework deployed on the JD App that addresses above challenges within a single decoder-only architecture. For training objective, we propose Page-wise NTP task, which supervises over an entire interaction page rather than each interacted item individually, providing denser gradient signal and resolving the one-to-many ambiguity of point-wise training. On the prefilling side, an asymmetric linear Token Merger compresses multi-token Semantic IDs in the prompt while preserving full-resolution decoding, reducing input length by ~2X with negligible accuracy loss. To further align outputs with user satisfaction, we introduce GRPO-SR, a reinforcement learning method that pairs Group Relative Policy Optimization with NLL regularization for training stability, and employs Hybrid Rewards combining a dense reward model with a relevance gate to mitigate reward hacking. In month-long online A/B tests serving production traffic, GenRec achieves 9.5% improvement in click count and 8.7% in transaction count over the existing pipeline.

</details>


### [136] [Metric-agnostic Learning-to-Rank via Boosting and Rank Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15101)
*Camilo Gomez,Pengyang Wang,Yanjie Fu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出一种新颖的列表式学习排序框架，通过可微排序损失函数解决传统方法对单一排序指标的依赖问题，实现高效且泛化性强的排序优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前学习排序方法依赖于单一前缀排序指标（如NDCG或MAP），存在两个主要问题：1）非可微问题：直接优化排序函数本质上是非平滑的，导致训练过程不稳定且低效；2）排序效用有限：基于单一指标优化难以泛化到其他感兴趣的排序指标。

Method: 提出新颖的列表式学习排序框架：1）设计新的可微排序损失函数，结合排序算子的平滑近似和每个查询的平均均方损失；2）采用梯度提升机来最小化针对每个列表提出的损失函数。

Result: 大量实验结果表明，该方法在信息检索指标上优于当前最先进方法，同时保持了相似的效率。

Conclusion: 提出的框架通过可微排序损失函数有效解决了传统学习排序方法的局限性，实现了更高效、更泛化的排序优化，为信息检索系统提供了更好的解决方案。

Abstract: Learning-to-Rank (LTR) is a supervised machine learning approach that constructs models specifically designed to order a set of items or documents based on their relevance or importance to a given query or context. Despite significant success in real-world information retrieval systems, current LTR methods rely on one prefix ranking metric (e.g., such as Normalized Discounted Cumulative Gain (NDCG) or Mean Average Precision (MAP)) for optimizing the ranking objective function. Such metric-dependent setting limits LTR methods from two perspectives: (1) non-differentiable problem: directly optimizing ranking functions over a given ranking metric is inherently non-smooth, making the training process unstable and inefficient; (2) limited ranking utility: optimizing over one single metric makes it difficult to generalize well to other ranking metrics of interest. To address the above issues, we propose a novel listwise LTR framework for efficient and generalizable ranking purpose. Specifically, we propose a new differentiable ranking loss that combines a smooth approximation to the ranking operator with the average mean square loss per query. Then, we adapt gradient-boosting machines to minimize our proposed loss with respect to each list, a novel contribution. Finally, extensive experimental results confirm that our method outperforms the current state-of-the-art in information retrieval measures with similar efficiency.

</details>
